# Swirly Drums

A normalized mirror of **Swirly Drums** by **Karoryfer Samples**: 4,796 brush-played drum samples, renamed to one shared convention, with an Opus twin of every sample.

## Why this repository exists

This is a good-will mirror that makes the pack easy to fetch in one place and in one naming scheme. It is **not** the author's repository; the author's own download remains the canonical source. Every source sample is copied byte-for-byte and renamed only; the Opus files are an additional convenience copy.

## What is here

- **4,796 samples** — 1,692 dry, 1,552 wet, 1,552 wetter.
- **Total source bytes:** 1,820,545,668 bytes (1,820.546 MB, 1.821 GB).
- **Total Opus bytes:** 222,309,181 bytes (222.309 MB).
- **Source format:** WAV, 44100 Hz, 16-bit PCM. Dry samples are mono (1 channel); wet and wetter samples are stereo (2 channels).
- **Opus twin:** 160 kbit/s VBR, 20 ms frames, 48 kHz (Opus always resamples).

## Naming

```
<pack>_<piece>_<articulation>_<perspective>_vl<NN>_rr<N>[_<take>].<codec>
```

All fields are lowercase ASCII joined by `_`. `vl01` is the softest dynamic layer, ascending. `rr` is the round robin, 1-based. `take` appears only where the same hit was recorded through more than one mic or side.

**Pieces used:** `kick1`, `snare1`, `tom1`, `tom2`, `tom3`, `tom4`, `tom5`, `hihat`, `crash1`, `ride1`, `china1`, `splash1`, `cowbell1`, `bongo1`, `bongo2`, `pedal`, `cajon`, `darbouka`, `djembe`.

**Articulations used:** `hit`, `center`, `edge`, `closed`, `quarter`, `half`, `open`, `tight`, `loose`, `foot`, `footsplash`, `pedal`, `broken`, `buzz`, `dig`, `stir`, `flutter`, `brush`, `bass`.

**Perspectives:** `dry` (close mics), `wet` and `wetter` (two room blends).

## How it was made

Every source sample was copied unchanged into `source/` under its new name. There was no resampling, normalisation, trimming or other alteration of the source bytes.

Each Opus twin was encoded once from the source WAV:

```sh
opusenc --bitrate 160 --vbr --comp 10 --framesize 20 "<source>" "opus/<stem>.opus"
```

`opusenc` from opus-tools 0.2 (libopus 1.6.1); audio probed with ffprobe 8.0.1.

## Removal

To have this mirror removed, write to **samples@karoryfer.com** (the contact address the pack itself gives); removal is immediate.
